<!-- page 1 -->
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
Tuesday, June 14, 2016 
 
PRESENT:  Robert Black, Supervisor; Steve Williams, Supervisor; Jesse Thompson, Vice-Chairman; and Jason 
Whiting, Chairman were present in person.  Dawnafe Whitesinger, Supervisor, was not present as she was 
attending United State of Women in Washington D.C. 
9:09 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation were led by Supervisor Black. 
CALL TO THE PUBLIC: None. 
CONSENT AGENDA:  a) Minutes: May 24, 2016  b) Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000 May 2016  c) 
Constable Reports:  Show Low Precinct #5, May; and Pinetop-Lakeside Precinct #6, May 2016 d) Justice Court 
Reports:  Holbrook Precinct #1, May; Winslow Precinct #2, May; Snowflake Precinct #3, May; Kayenta Precinct 
#4, May; Show Low Precinct #5, May; and Pinetop -Lakeside Precinct #6, May 2016  e) Clerk of t he Superior 
Court Report May 2016 f) Juvenile Probation Report May 2016 g) Approval of 1 Back Tax Land Parcel sold over 
the counter in the amount of $980.33 h) Amendment to the Intergovernmental Agreement for Dispatch Services 
for the City of Holbrook from  $100,000.00 to $140,000.00 annually  i) Conduct Elections for Town of Taylor, 
City of Show Low, Town of Snowflake and Town of Pinetop -Lakeside on August 30 and November 8, 2016  j) 
Arizona Criminal Justice Commission Byrne Grant #DC -17-009 for $234,416.00 f or operation of the Major 
Crimes Apprehension team: Supervisor Williams made a motion to authorize the Chairman to sign the items in 
the consent agenda; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
ADMINISTRATIVE SERVICES:  
a. Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions: Katherine Wright reviewed the 23-item personnel 
action report. Supervisor Thompson made a motion to approve the Personnel Action Report as presented ; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Williams. Vote unanimous approving the motion.   
b. Recognition of Mr. James Menlove and the Administrative Services Team for receiving the Distinguished 
Budget Presentation Award by the Government Finance Officers Association of the United States and Canada 
for the Fiscal Year 2015 : Bryan Layton introduced the new Public Works Director , Glenn Kephart , and 
highlighted Mr. Kephart’s experience. Mr. Kephart addressed the Board. Supervisor Williams indicated that 
he is excited to work with Mr. Kephart and welcomed him to the team. Mr. Layton recognized James Menlove 
and the Administrative Services Team  for receiving the Distinguished Budget Presentation Award by the 
Government Finance Officers Association of the United States and Canada for Fiscal year 2015. Mr. Layton 
introduced the Administrative Services Team and gave a special thanks to Olivia Todd for the time she spent 
on the presentation of the budget book. Supervisor Williams  thanked Mr. Menlove and his team.   He 
expressed his appreciation for the professional and competent work that they do. Chairman Whiting 
recognized the caliber of talent and commitment the Administrative Services team has for helping the citizens 
who live here. Supervisor Thompson indicated that the award was well deserved.   
c. Consideration and possible adoption of the proposed Tentative Budget for Fiscal Year 2016 -17 for Navajo 
County: Bryan Layton reviewed the proposed tentative budget.  Supervisor Williams made a motion  to 
adopt the proposed Tentative Budget for Fiscal Year 2016 -17 for Navajo County ; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Thompson . Vote unanimous approving the motion. The Board of Supervisors thanked the 
Administrative Services Team, the elected officials and the department directors for their partnership in trying 
to find the best solutions to use the taxpayer’s monies to benefit the communities as a whole.

<!-- page 2 -->
d. Board of Directors Sessions for consideration and possible adoption of tentative Fiscal Year 2016-17 budgets: 
Supervisor Williams made a motion to enter into the Board of Directors Sessions of the Navajo County 
Public Health Services District.  Supervisor Williams made a motion to withdraw his motion to enter into 
the Board of Directors Sessions of the Navajo County Public Health Services District; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Thompson.  Vote unanimous approving the motion.  Supervisor Williams made a motion to 
simultaneously enter into the Board of Directors Sessions for the Navajo County Public Health Servic es 
District, Navajo County Library District, Navajo County Flood Control District including LCR Flood Control 
Zone, White Mountain Lakes County Recreation Improvement District, Silver Creek County Road 
Improvement District, Victory Heights Road Maintenance  District, Fawnbrook Domestic Wastewater 
Improvement District, Sutter Drive Improvement District, Scott’s Pine Meadow County Road Improvement 
District, Shumway Road Improvement District, Bucking Horse Improvement District, Hilltop Drive 
Improvement District, Mountain View Improvement District, North Whistle Stop Loop Improvement District, 
and Joseph City Street Lighting Improvement District ; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson.  Vote 
unanimous approving the motion.  Bryan Layton reviewed the budgets.  Supervisor Williams made a motion 
to adopt the tentative Fiscal Year 2016 -17 budgets for the districts referenced below; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion . Supervisor Williams made a motion  to 
return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
i)      Navajo County Public Health Services District 
ii)      Navajo County Library District 
iii)      Navajo County Flood Control District including LCR Flood Control Zone 
iv)      White Mountain Lakes County Recreation Improvement District 
v)      Silver Creek County Road Improvement District 
vi)      Victory Heights Road Maintenance District 
vii)       Fawnbrook Domestic Wastewater Improvement District 
viii) Sutter Drive Improvement District 
ix)       Scott’s Pine Meadow County Road Improvement District 
x)       Shumway Road Improvement District 
xi)       Bucking Horse Improvement District 
xii)       Hilltop Drive Improvement District 
xiii) Mountain View Improvement District 
xiv) North Whistle Stop Loop Improvement District 
xv)       Joseph City Street Lighting Improvement District 
PUSH PROGRAM:  
a. Presentation to May Fitness Hero of the Month, Leah Thomas and June Fitness Hero of the Month, Charlotte 
Yazzie: Callie Jensen stated that Leah Thomas was the May Fitness Hero.  Ms. Jensen recognized Charlotte 
Yazzie as June Fitness Hero of the Month. Ms. Jensen spoke about the activities that Ms. Yazzie has 
participated in and the measures she has taken for better health.  
b. Information on 2016 Navajo County Health Fair “Home Run to Health”: Ms. Jensen spoke about the Health 
Fair being held tomorrow from 9:00 a.m. to 2:00 p.m. She spoke of the vendors who will be participating and 
that biometrics will be done throughout the County. She spoke of the Employee Appreciation Luncheon. 
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  
a. Consideration and possible acceptance of the resignation of Lita Dixon as Constable of Precinct #4, effective 
June 1, 2016 :  Melissa Buckley discussed the item.  Supervisor Black made a motion to accept the 
resignation of Lita Dixon as Constable of Precinct #4, effective June 1, 2016; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Williams.  Vote unanimous approving the motion.

<!-- page 3 -->
b. Consideration and possible appointment of Constable of Precinct #4 to fill the remain der of the unexpired 
term of the vacant office:  Melissa Buckley stated that she received two letters:  One from Constable Dixon 
recommending the appointment of Jonathan Derrick Nez, who is a certified registered Democrat within the 
precinct and one from Supervisor Black recommending the appointment of Elizabeth Whitethorne-Benally, 
who is also a certified registered Democrat within the precinct.  Supervisor Black made a motion to appoint 
Elizabeth Whitethorne-Benally as constable of Precinct #4 to fill the  remainder of the unexpired term of the 
vacant office; motion seconded by Supervisor Williams. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
PUBLIC FIDUCIARY: 
a. Consideration and possible acceptance of resignation of Sherry Reed, Public Fiduciary effective June 23, 
2016: Sherry Reed spoke of her time with Navajo County and asked the Board’s consideration and acceptance 
of her retirement.  Supervisor Williams made a motion  to accept the resignation of Sherry Reed, Public 
Fiduciary, effective June 23, 2016; motion secon ded by Supervisor Black. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion. 
b. Consideration and possible acceptance of appointment of N. Brent Turley as successor Navajo County Public 
Fiduciary effective June 23, 2016: Sherry Reed recommended the appointment of N. Brent Turley as successor 
Navajo County Public Fiduciary effective June 23, 2016, and stated that Mr. Turley has been with the office 
for 15 years as the accountant. Supervisor Williams made a motion to accept the appointment of N. Brent 
Turley as successor Navajo County Public Fiduciary effective June 23, 2016; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Ms.Reed stated that her office has a 15 -year succession 
plan.  Mr. Turley thanked the Board for the opportunity.  He spoke of the amazing job that Sherry has done  
noting that she has served on the AAOC Board for the last two or three years and is well respected.  He stated 
that Sherry has been great for Navajo County , and it has been a pleasure working with her. He stated that 
Navajo County is one of the most respected Public Fiduciarie s in the State, and he is looking forward to 
keeping the legacy going.  
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:   Presentation to retiring employee, Sherry Reed, Public Fiduciary : Chairman 
Whiting and the Board of Sup ervisors presented Sherry Reed with a clock and certificate reminding her of the 
time she spent serving the citizens of Navajo County and wished her well.  Chairman Whiting stated that there 
will be a retirement party for Ms. Reed immediately following the Board meeting. 
COUNTY ATTORNEY:  
a. Consideration and possible acceptance of the Arizona Criminal Justice Commission Victim Assistance Grant 
#VA-17-051: Melissa Buckley discussed the item.  Supervisor Williams made a motion to accept the 
Arizona Criminal Justice Commission Victim Assistance Grant #VA -17-051; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Black. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible acceptance of the Arizona Criminal Justice Commission Drug, Gang, and Violent 
Crime Control Grant #DC -17-029:  Melissa Buckley discussed the item.  Supervisor Williams made a 
motion to accept the Arizona Criminal Justice Commission Drug, Gang, and Violent Crime Control Grant 
#DC-17-029; motion seconded by Supervisor Black. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
EMERGENCY MANAGEMENT: 
a. Consideration and possible approval of the 5 -year Public Health Emergency Preparedness Grant : Jennifer 
Flake discussed the item . Supervisor Williams made a motion to approve the 5 -year Public He alth 
Emergency Preparedness Grant; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.

<!-- page 4 -->
b. Consideration and possible approval of an Intergovernmental Agreement between Northland Pioneer College 
and Navajo County Emergency Management for the use of the Ready Navajo County emergency alert system: 
Mary Springer reviewed the Intergovernmental Agreement between Northland Pioneer College and Navajo 
County Emergency  Management for the use of the Ready Navajo County emergency alert system .  
Supervisor Thompson made a motion to approve the Intergovernmental Agreement between Northland 
Pioneer College and Navajo County Emergency Management for the use of the Ready Navajo County 
emergency alert system; motion seconded by Supervisor Williams. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
c. Consideration and possible approval of an Intergovernmental Agreement between the City of Show Low and 
Navajo County Emergency Management for the use of the Ready Navajo County emergency alert system : 
Mary Springer reviewed the Intergovernmental Agreement between the City of Show Low and Navajo County 
Emergency Management for the use of the Ready Navajo County emergency alert system. Ms. Springer 
indicated that she is excited about these partnerships. Supervisor Williams made a motion to approve the 
Intergovernmental Agreement between the City of Show Low and Navajo County Emergency Management 
for the use of the Ready Navajo County emergency alert system; motion seconded by Supervisor  Thompson. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion . Chairman Whiting spoke about the fire that broke out in Yarnelle 
and that a system such as this was used to immediately notify the citizens.  
d. Consideration and possible approval of implementing fire restrictions for Zones 1, 2 and 3 in Navajo County: 
Mary Springer discussed the partnerships that emergency management has and spoke of the fire restrictions 
call that she has weekly. She indicated that last Wednesday, the group indicated that it is time to implement 
Stage One fire restrictions.  Ms. Springer asked that this go into effect at 8:00 a.m., June 15, 2016, to go along 
with the other entities. She stated there were 25 abandoned active campfires this past weekend.  Supervisor 
Williams asked how we get the word out to our  citizens if the restrictions are approved . She indicated that 
she has contacted Navajo County’s Communications Manager, the partners have prepared their press releases, 
and they are also partnered with State Forestry at firerestrictions.az.us, so information is posted and available 
online, print media, and public service announcements .  Discussion was held regarding fires in the area and 
red flag warnings. Ms. Springer discussed what the public can and cannot do in Stage One, and stated that the 
public will be educated.  Supervisor Williams asked if the fire restrictions were lifted, then the issue would 
have to come back to the Board.  Ms. Springer stated that she believes that they can be rescinded and then 
come back to the Board for ratification.  Supervisor Thompson made a motion to approve implementing 
fire restrictions for Zones 1, 2 and 3 in Navajo County ; motion seconded by Supervisor Black . Vote 
unanimous approving the motion.  
PUBLIC WORKS:  NAVAJO COUNTY FLOOD CONTROL DISTRICT:  Board of Directors  Session: 
Supervisor Williams made a motion to enter into the Navajo County Flood Control District Board of Directors 
Session; motion seconded by Supervisor Black.  Vote unanimous approving the motion.  Consideration and 
possible approval of an easement of a drainage easement across Assessor’s Parcel Number 411 -60-062 for 
$15,000 as part of regional drainage improvements for Pinetop country Club:  Bill Bess discussed the item. 
Supervisor Williams made a motion to approve an easement of a drainage easement ac ross Assessor’s Parcel 
Number 411-60-062 for $15,000 as part of regional drainage improvements for Pinetop Country Club ; motion 
seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion . Supervisor Williams made a 
motion to return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion. 
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE:  Report from Board members, County Attorney and Assistant County 
Manager: Supervisor Black gave a report for District I.  He reported that he attended the Regional NACo event 
in Jackson Hole , Wyoming.  He stated he came away with a lot of ideas.  He spoke of the issues of roads  
throughout the region.  He thanked Deputy Roy Lawrence and Sheriff Clark for bringing up some of their staff

<!-- page 5 -->
to the Rock the Ca nyon event in Shonto.  Supervisor Williams gave a report for District IV.  He spoke about 
today being Flag Day, noting that 239 years ago the flag was adopted.  He spoke about a local Boy Scout troup 
to whom he donates each year, and they put a flag outside his house.  He stated that today is the 241st birthday for 
the Army. He indicated that he was glad to see Mrs. Hill in the Board meeting today. He asked that we keep Paul 
Watson in our prayers as he lost his mother last night. He congratulated Seth Jayne  on his marriage this past 
weekend. Supervisor Thompson gave a report for District II.  He stated that he met with the Rotary Club in 
Phoenix and talked about assisting the Hopi Tribe in putting up some recreational facilities.  He indicated that he 
attended a meeting in Window Rock about roads. He stated that he has had several meetings with the communities 
and talked about their various issues and projects that they have . He spoke about a meeting in Window Rock 
regarding Keeping our Kids Safe and how we need to pull together.  He further spoke about the Winslow Levee 
public information meetings. Chairman Whiting gave a report for District III.  He expressed his appreciation for 
the community that lwe have in Navajo County. He spoke about the commitment tha t many of us have for the 
community that we live in. He stated that our hearts and prayers are with those affected by the massacre in Florida.  
He is grateful for the many blessings that we have, the men and women that serve our country and our 
communities. He thanked the Board for how they represent their communities.  Supervisor Black invited the 
public out to the Western Navajo Agency Council meeting on Saturday, June 18 , at 9:00 a.m. , at the 
Accommodation School in Holbrook.  
ADJOURN: 10:10 a.m.  Supervisor Williams made a motion to adjourn; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Thompson.  Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
APPROVED:       DATED: June 28, 2016 
 
Jason E. Whiting, Chairman 
Navajo County Board of Supervisors 
 
 
ATTEST: 
 
Melissa W. Buckley, Clerk 
Navajo County Board of Supervisors
